Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally gen","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T10:57:06Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12426.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是一个很具体但常被低估的问题：模型单独遵守一条指令通常不难，但当多个约束需要同时满足时，性能会如何退化，退化是平滑的还是会突然塌陷，塌陷来自约束之间的相互干扰，还是只是单条约束可靠性不足在组合时被乘法放大。作者把问题限定在“可确定性验证的多约束满足”，试图给出比以往 benchmark 更干净的定量答案。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接 recipe 贡献，但对能力评估和训练目标设计有明确启发。第一，它把多约束遵循失败解释为近似独立错误的累积，而不是复杂的 pairwise interference，这会影响人如何设计 instruction tuning、constraint-aware decoding 和 verifier-based training。第二，它给出一个较清晰的功能形式：轻微的 per-constraint 退化，在组合层面会迅速变成整体成功率崩塌。这对理解“为什么模型看起来会做，但一上真实复杂系统就不稳”很有帮助。第三，它用全确定性 verifier 避开了 LLM judge 在高组合度场景下自身失真的问题，因此 benchmark 信号更可信。对基座研究员来说，这篇文章更像一个能力测量工具和现象学刻画，而不是新训练方法。","technical_approach":"作者提出 Constraint Saturation Evaluation（CSE）。核心设计有两点。第一，所有约束都必须可被确定性程序验证，不依赖 LLM judge。这样评测器不会在高 k 组合场景下和被测模型一起失真。第二，benchmark 不是固定几种组合，而是系统地扫描同时约束数 k，从 1 到 12，观察性能曲线和失效结构。CSE 包含 36 类约束，覆盖 8 个处理维度，从局部词法模式到文档级结构，再到关系推理和自指约束。每个 verifier 不只给二值通过/失败，还给连续分数 s∈[0,1]，便于分析部分满足。作者还专门做了 composability vetting，避免某个约束天然锁死多个输出维度，导致高 k 时 benchmark 本身带入结构性偏差。实验分析的关键不是简单报 pass rate，而是拆成三层：一是 per-constraint pass rate 随 k 的变化；二是 probe-level joint success 的塌陷速度；三是 failure correlation，也就是不同约束失败是否强相关。结论是失败之间相关性很弱，平均 φ 只有 +0.067，因此联合成功率大致可以由单约束成功率相乘来预测。作者进一步分析不同约束类型的退化差异，发现需要在生成过程中持续维护的结构性约束，比局部词法约束退化更快；而接近一次性二元判断的约束则相对稳定。这种分层解释比“模型不会多任务”要细得多。","experiments":"实验规模上，CSE 覆盖 15 个模型、8 个家族、36 种约束类型、k=1 到 12，共 369,753 次约束检查。最核心的定量结果有四个。第一，per-constraint pass rate 可以被一个简单乘法模型很好拟合：72.0% × 0.922^(k−1)，held-out MAE 只有 0.2 个百分点。这说明退化不是杂乱无章，而是相当规则。第二，虽然单条约束的通过率下降看起来不算剧烈，但联合成功率会很快跌穿地板：到 k=9 时，probe-level success 低于 2%；一个模型在 k=8 时单约束通过率约 41%，但八条全满足只有 5.7%。第三，即使最强模型，整体成功率跌破 50% 也只需要 7 条约束；15 个模型里有 12 个在 3 条或更少约束时就跌破 50%。第四，结构性约束每增加一条约束时损失的基线能力约为词法约束的 2.0 倍，95% CI 为 [1.9, 2.3]；而作者定义的 comprehension-maintenance gap 与退化率显著相关，ρ=-0.584。也就是说，越依赖生成过程中持续跟踪和维护的约束，越容易先坏。这个结果对训练很有启发：如果想提升复杂指令遵循，单纯增加约束种类未必有效，更关键的是提高每条约束在长生成过程中的稳定执行率。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先看 Introduction 和 Section 4。Introduction 把问题、已有 benchmark 的缺口、以及为什么必须去掉 LLM judge 讲得比较清楚。Section 4 是整篇最有信息量的部分，里面有退化函数、联合成功率塌陷、以及 failure correlation 的核心结果。然后回看 CSE 的 benchmark 设计，重点看 36 类约束如何筛选、为什么要做 composability analysis、以及 continuous score 的定义。若只想抓住主要结论，30 分钟左右足够。若想把它用于训练或评测设计，需要细读 Appendix K 一类的 verifier vetting 和约束构造细节，确认 benchmark 没有把不必要的耦合带进来。阅读时建议一直带着一个问题：这里测到的是“模型不会理解多个要求”，还是“模型理解了但在生成时维持不住”？作者的数据更支持后者。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a narrow but important failure mode: language models can usually satisfy one instruction at a time, but what happens when many explicit constraints must be satisfied jointly? The authors ask whether performance degrades smoothly or collapses sharply, whether some constraints fail earlier than others, and whether the collapse is caused by pairwise interference between constraints or by the accumulation of mostly independent errors.","importance":"This is not a pretraining recipe paper, but it matters for capability measurement and training design. First, it argues that multi-constraint failure is largely an accumulation problem rather than a rich interference problem. That changes how one might think about instruction tuning, verifier-based training, and constrained decoding. Second, it gives a concrete functional form for degradation: modest per-constraint decay compounds into severe joint failure. That helps explain why models can look competent on isolated instructions yet become unreliable in real systems with many simultaneous requirements. Third, by using deterministic verifiers throughout, it avoids the circularity of LLM judges failing in the same high-composition regime they are supposed to evaluate. For foundation-model researchers, the main value is as a clean measurement framework and a useful phenomenology of instruction-following limits.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"$21","reading_guide":"For a skim, start with the introduction and Section 4. The introduction clearly motivates why existing benchmarks are insufficient, especially when they rely on LLM judges. Section 4 contains the main empirical substance: the degradation law, the collapse in joint success, and the weak co-failure correlations. After that, read the benchmark design section to understand how the 36 constraints were selected, why composability analysis matters, and how the continuous compliance scores are defined. If the goal is just to extract the main result, about 30 minutes should be enough. If the goal is to reuse the benchmark or adapt its ideas for training, the verifier-vetting and constraint-construction appendices are worth reading carefully. A useful reading lens is this: is the benchmark showing that models fail to understand multiple requirements, or that they understand them but cannot reliably maintain them during generation? The evidence in the paper points more strongly to the second interpretation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"verifier 设计干净，相变结论对指令数据构造和输出格式训练有直接意义；只看§3的相变曲线和k=8表格即可。","en":"Clean verifier-based setup; the phase-transition result matters for instruction-data design and schema-following training; just read §3 curves and the k=8 table."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Mariya I. Most games include a creative mode: a sandbox with similar mechanics but a separate step budget and different initial state, allowing free experimentation without directly leaking solutions. Games avoid large 2D grids and visual layout, removing visuospatial confounds, and full trajectories usually fit within current frontier context windows, so the benchmark tests in-context discovery rather than long-horizon memory or tooling. We introduc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T13:24:47Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13221.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有 LLM 推理评测要么只看最终答案正确率，要么只分析单条 CoT 轨迹的正确性、简洁性与逻辑一致性，无法衡量模型在多分支对抗性问题中如何规划搜索路径、比较候选分支、在不同分支间分配推理资源——即搜索组织能力。","importance":"提出了一个新的过程级评测维度 Search Efficiency（搜索效率），把推理评测从单条轨迹质量推进到多分支资源分配的组织能力，为研究搜索增强推理、o1 类扩展思考、过程监督奖励模型等方向提供了可控且可验证的基准环境。","technical_approach":"构建 TsuGO 基准，基于围棋死活题（tsumego）构造封闭、可验证、天然带对抗结构的问题空间。每个问题满足三个条件：相关棋子局部化、正确首着有限且不依赖劫争、解答经专家与围棋引擎交叉验证。问题提供 5 种模态输入：符号坐标、网格矩阵、符号+网格、视觉图像、符号+视觉，覆盖纯文本与多模态模型。评测框架将自由格式 CoT 解析为过程搜索树，定义两个核心指标：SearchE（搜索效率，衡量推理资源是否集中到正确分支）与 TokenE（按 token 开销归一化的正确率），并辅以轨迹级与规模级诊断指标。","experiments":"实验覆盖前沿闭源与开源 LLM，对比基线为神经引导的 KataGo。核心发现：更强模型的优势主要来自更早提出正确候选着法，并持续在有效分支上投入资源，而非单纯减少 token 数量；SearchE 与模型能力等级的相关性高于 TokenE，说明搜索组织是独立于答案正确率与 token 效率的能力维度；即使是最强前沿 LLM，在 TsuGO 上的表现仍显著落后于 KataGo，基准远未饱和。","reading_guide":"泛读约 25 分钟。重点看 §3 基准构造的三个筛选条件与多模态输入设计，§4 搜索树解析方法与 SearchE/TokenE 的定义，以及 §5 中不同模型在分支资源分配上的行为差异。附录中的题目来源与可追溯性说明可快速浏览。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Current LLM reasoning evaluations either measure only final-answer accuracy or analyze the correctness, conciseness, and logical coherence of a single CoT trajectory. They cannot capture how models plan search paths, compare candidate branches, and allocate reasoning resources across competing branches in adversarial multi-branch problems — that is, search organization capability.","importance":"TsuGO introduces Search Efficiency as a new process-level evaluation dimension, pushing reasoning assessment beyond single-trajectory quality toward multi-branch resource allocation. It provides a controlled and verifiable benchmark environment for research on search-augmented reasoning, extended-thinking paradigms, and process-supervised reward models.","technical_approach":"TsuGO is built on Go life-and-death (tsumego) problems, which form a closed, verifiable, and inherently adversarial solution space. Each retained problem satisfies three criteria: locality of relevant stones, finite correct first moves independent of ko, and expert- plus engine-verified ground truth. Problems are rendered in five modalities — symbolic coordinates, grid matrices, symbolic+grid, visual images, and symbolic+visual — to support both text-only and vision-language models. The evaluation framework parses free-form CoT into process search trees and reports two core metrics: SearchE (search efficiency, measuring whether reasoning resources concentrate on correct branches) and TokenE (accuracy normalized by token cost), plus trajectory- and scale-level diagnostics.","experiments":"Experiments cover frontier closed-source and open-source LLMs, with neural-guided KataGo as the reference baseline. Key findings: stronger models outperform weaker ones primarily by proposing correct candidates earlier and sustaining effort on productive branches, rather than by simply using fewer tokens; SearchE correlates better with model capability tier than TokenE, indicating that search organization is a distinct dimension from outcome accuracy and token efficiency; even top-tier LLMs still lag far behind KataGo on TsuGO, leaving the benchmark far from saturated.","reading_guide":"Skim in roughly 25 minutes. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. 工作、偷偷搬设备等），验证器必须全部拒绝同时放行诚实参考。GDN 反向部分：手写原生 Blackwell tcgen05/tensor-memory 训练反向，包含其他实现仍留在 Triton fallback 的 reverse-state 阶段，正确处理了 open #904 bug 背后的 tensor-memory 约束。","experiments":"审计 Dr. 在无容忍门下即破损，62.1% 携带契约违反；与 KernelBench 自有 harness 的差分显示标准测试认证了 1,487 个破损 kernel，反向只有 14 个。GDN 反向 kernel 对 fp64 oracle 验证到 3.3×10⁻³，bit-for-bit 确定性，支持五个 GDN 家族成员的一次反向，正确处理 tensor-memory 约束。附录还报告六个 contract-verified 的 Mamba-3 kernel（在 Blackwell 上绕过 #904）、用这些 kernel 端到端训练的 1.1B 模型、以及从零搭建的 GRPO 自动调优系统。所有定量声明有固定软件环境下的已提交 artifact，单行复现只需公开验证器和公开 MIT corpus。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：Introduction This mismatch is particularly acute for video, where generators must jointly model appearance, motion, and long-range temporal dependencies, and the poor semantic organization of conventional latent spaces limits both generation quality and training efficiency.","importance":"This work extends the Representation Autoencoder (RAE) paradigm from images to video, directly using features from frozen vision foundation models as the generative latent space. It provides a new technical path for pretraining paradigms that unify visual understanding and generation, as well as unified multimodal modeling. On the decoder side, a spatiotemporal Transformer decoder with 3D RoPE, combined with a multi-frame unpatchify layer that maps each temporal latent step to multiple consecutive frames, reconstructs temporally coherent videos from compressed latents. The feature encoder remains frozen throughout, so the final latent space retains the semantic organization of the pretrained vision foundation model. heavily on designer-specified privileged artifacts (e.g., answers, feedback, skills, or trajectories), limiting the end-to-end learnability and scalability required for cont","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T10:05:51Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13040.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决 on-policy self-distillation 中 privileged context 依赖人工设计的问题。已有 OPSD 通常把答案、反馈、成功轨迹、反思文本或技能标签作为 teacher 的额外上下文，但这些格式预先规定了 teacher 能看到什么。LOPD 的目标是让 privileged context 变成可学习的连续 latent tokens，由经验数据端到端生成，并通过 teacher 的 token-level 分布监督 student。","importance":"对预训练和 post-train 接口的启发在于：它把经验学习中的“监督信号形式”从人工文本模板转成可优化的 latent substrate。虽然论文场景是 agent 和 on-policy distillation，但核心问题接近 RL/SFT 数据如何转化为密集训练信号。相比 RLVR 只用稀疏可验证奖励，LOPD 试图把历史经验压缩成 teacher 可用的连续条件，使 student 在自己实际访问的状态上获得更细粒度的分布监督。这个方向对 continual self-improvement、训练信号压缩、latent memory-conditioned teacher 都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"流程保留标准 OPSD 的结构。student 根据任务和交互历史采样自己的 trajectory；teacher 访问同一批 visited prefixes，并输出 token-level distribution；student 用 reverse-KL 向 teacher 蒸馏。关键变化是 teacher 的 privileged context 不再来自手写规则，而是由 composer 从检索到的 past experiences 中生成一组连续 latent context tokens。composer 和 student 通过同一训练目标联合优化，因此 latent context 学习保留哪些经验信息能让 teacher 给出更有用的监督。为避免 teacher 退化成容易被 student 匹配但没有额外信息的分布，作者加入 privileged-margin constraint，要求 teacher 相对 student 保持可验证的 log-probability advantage。训练结束后只保留 student，推理时不需要经验库、检索器、composer 或 latent context。","experiments":"论文报告在 3 个模型 backbone 和 7 个 benchmark 上验证 LOPD。结果显示 LOPD 优于 RLVR 和代表性 OPSD 方法；相较 GRPO 和 Skill-SD，使用不到 30% 的 rollout budget 取得更好的结果。消融实验重点验证两个设计：第一，latent privileged context 需要联合学习，固定或手工设计的 context 效果较弱；第二，privileged-margin constraint 有助于保持 teacher 的信息优势，减少 teacher-student 分布塌缩。由于给出的全文片段未包含完整表格，具体 benchmark 名称和每项绝对分数无法确认。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 introduction 中对 OPSD、RLVR、privileged context 的问题定义，再看 method 中 latent composer、reverse-KL distillation 和 privileged-margin constraint 的公式。实验部分优先看三类对比：与 RLVR 的样本效率差异，与其他 OPSD 变体的对比，以及 composer / margin 的消融。预计阅读 30 分钟。如果关注 RL pretrain 或持续学习，值得重点看 latent context 是否真的学到可迁移的经验压缩，而不是只在固定 benchmark 上提供额外条件信息。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a limitation of on-policy self-distillation: the privileged context given to the self-teacher is usually hand-designed. Prior OPSD methods condition the teacher on answers, feedback, successful trajectories, textual reflections, or skill annotations. LOPD instead makes the privileged context a learnable continuous latent substrate generated from past experience.","importance":"The idea is relevant to the interface between reinforcement learning, distillation, and continual improvement. Rather than treating experience as sparse rewards or manually formatted textual artifacts, LOPD learns a compact conditioning signal that helps a self-teacher produce dense token-level supervision on the student’s own trajectories. This is a useful lens for thinking about how post-training signals can be compressed, reused, and internalized by the policy without requiring extra context at inference time.","technical_approach":"LOPD keeps the standard OPSD loop. The student samples on-policy trajectories from the task and interaction history. The teacher then reevaluates the same visited prefixes and provides token-level distributions. The student is trained with reverse-KL distillation toward the teacher. The main change is how the teacher receives privileged information: a composer retrieves prior experiences and maps them into continuous latent context tokens. The composer is optimized jointly with the distillation objective, so it learns which parts of experience are useful for teacher supervision. A privileged-margin constraint requires the teacher to maintain a verifiable log-probability advantage over the student, preventing the learned context from collapsing into an uninformative condition. At inference, only the student is kept; no retrieval module, composer, database, or latent context is needed.","experiments":"The paper reports experiments over three model backbones and seven benchmarks. LOPD outperforms RLVR and representative OPSD baselines, and achieves better results than GRPO and Skill-SD with less than 30% of their rollout budget. Ablations focus on the need to learn the privileged context jointly and the role of the privileged-margin constraint. While this improves pass@1 (single-shot accuracy), it reduces solution space coverage, often leading to worse pass@k performance than the base model for larger k, and prevents models from fully leveraging test-time scaling via diverse sampling.","importance":"This work identifies a core tradeoff in existing RL post-training pipelines: the tension between optimizing single-sample accuracy and preserving solution diversity. For domains that rely on test-time compute scaling (mathematical discovery, coding, open scientific problem solving) where performance depends on finding at least one correct solution across many samples, policy gradient methods like PPO/GRPO are misaligned with the end objective. Evolution strategies (ES), a gradient-free optimization method operating in parameter space, provides a viable alternative, adding empirical evidence for better post-training objective design and pretrain-posttrain interface design.","technical_approach":"The work first isolates the fundamental mechanistic difference between RL and ES: RL performs policy gradient updates in the token action space, directly increasing log probability of high-reward sequences and inherently sharpening the output distribution toward high-reward modes; ES performs population-based gradient-free optimization directly in weight space, generating a population of models via random parameter perturbations, updating parameters based on mean population reward, and steering parameters toward regions robust to perturbation without explicitly sharpening a single output mode, naturally preserving broad support over the solution space. The study systematically compares distributional properties and pass@k performance across model families and parameter scales.","experiments":"Experiments span multiple model families from 1.5B to 32B parameters under matched training compute budgets, with three core findings: 1) ES-trained models outperform RL-trained models of the same size on pass@k across all tested settings, with larger gaps as k increases; 2) ES-trained models have significantly lower KL divergence from the base model distribution than RL-trained models, retaining more low-probability but correct solution paths with no observable distribution collapse; 3) On mathematical reasoning benchmarks, ES-trained models paired with test-time sampling outperform RL-trained models trained with the same compute budget.","reading_guide":"Suitable for skimming. We present RoutePack, a hierarc","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T15:07:15Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12146.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DC","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"MoE 强化学习训练同时存在两类负载不均衡：数据并行侧由变长样本打包决定的 attention 计算，以及专家并行侧由 token 路由决定的专家计算。单独优化其中一侧，往往会把瓶颈转移到另一侧。","importance":"这是一个系统与训练编排层面的改进，直接面向 MoE RL 场景。对预训练研究的价值主要在于：当 MoE 模型用于 RL/post-training 时，rollout 阶段已经暴露了每个样本的长度和逐层专家需求，这些信号可以反过来用于训练时的布局决策，而不是只把数据打包当作预处理步骤。","technical_approach":"RoutePack 在一个 optimizer-step 窗口内分层规划。第一步根据 rollout replay 得到的聚合路由需求，逐层放置专家，降低样本重排无法消除的 EP 侧整体不均衡。第二步在专家放置固定后，把完整样本分配到 token 数受限的矩形执行行中，目标同时考虑 cell-local attention 开销和 shard-local 专家 rank 尾部负载。求解器采用 capacity-feasible fixed-row 构造、多种子初始化和并行 population annealing，并优先保证最小可行行数，再优化负载均衡。整个规划发生在 batch 封存后、训练准入前，以保持状态一致性。","experiments":"在 Tiny 和 Flash 两类模型上，相比基线，trainer 实测 token throughput 分别提升 8.85% 和 14.89%。论文还分析了 host-side planning 与 placement-aware state materialization 的重叠条件，以及规划使用的计算 proxy 与实测吞吐之间的关系。","reading_guide":"泛读即可。建议先看 Figure 1 理解 control plane/data plane，再看第 3 节目标函数如何把 attention 与 EP tail 合并，第 4 节分层求解和 CPU 规划不阻塞训练的条件。重点不是新模型结构，而是 MoE RL 中路由 replay 如何同时驱动专家放置和样本打包。预计 20–30 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"MoE reinforcement learning training faces two coupled load-balancing problems: attention load depends on how variable-length sequences are packed into data-parallel microbatches, while expert load depends on token routing across expert-parallel ranks. Optimizing either side alone can move the bottleneck to the other.","importance":"The work is primarily a systems and training-orchestration contribution for MoE RL. Its relevance to pretraining research is that rollout already reveals each sample’s length and layer-wise expert demand before the optimizer step. That signal can be used for training layout rather than treating packing as a routing-blind preprocessing stage.","technical_approach":"RoutePack uses a hierarchical planner over an optimizer-step window. It first places experts independently per layer using aggregate routing demand replayed from rollout, reducing EP imbalance that sample reordering cannot remove. With expert placement fixed, it then assigns complete samples to token-capped rectangular execution rows. The objective combines projected attention work with per-layer physical expert-rank tails, prioritizing the smallest feasible row count before balancing load. The solver uses capacity-feasible fixed-row construction, diverse seeds, and parallel population annealing. Planning runs after the batch is sealed and before training admission, preserving state consistency.","experiments":"On Tiny and Flash models, trainer-measured token throughput improves by 8.85% and 14.89% over the baseline. We study this refusal-to-richness trade-off in long-form hallucination RL. Instead of using global richness proxies such as length, claim count, detail, or pairwise relevance, we represent each question with a key-point rubric that specifies the required and optional information a useful answer should cover. These rubrics define coverage directly and are used bot","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-03T03:41:39Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12337.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是长文本幻觉 RL 里的一个老问题，但切得更具体：如果奖励只强调 grounding，模型很容易学会“少说少错”，答案变短、覆盖变差；如果只鼓励丰富度，又会把 unsupported 内容带回来。作者要解决的是如何在长答案训练中同时保住支持性和信息覆盖，而且覆盖不是用长度、claim 数这类粗代理，而是按题目定制的 key-point rubric 来定义。","importance":"它对预训练本身不是直接贡献，更偏 post-train / RL 信号设计，但和 pretrain 接口关系很紧。原因在于：这类工作揭示了奖励函数如何系统性改变模型的输出分布，尤其是把模型推向保守拒答还是完整作答。对做持续预训练、mid-training、合成监督信号、reward modeling 的人都有参考价值。真正有启发的点不是“rubric 比长度好”这么简单，而是说明长文本 RL 里需要把‘内容覆盖’显式结构化，否则 grounding reward 会诱导出错误的最优策略。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 richness 从全局代理变量改成 per-sample checklist。作者定义 rubric 不是参考答案，而是问题特定的信息清单，分 required 和 optional key points，并配一个一句话 completeness summary。模型回答的 richness 用 rubric coverage 来打分；grounding 则用句子级支持性信号，文中统一把 factuality 和 faithfulness 都纳入 grounding。\n\n论文比较了五类奖励，核心差别在于 grounding、rubric coverage 和 pairwise relevance 三种信号如何组合。文中强调几种组合方式的行为差异：纯 grounding 奖励会强烈压缩输出；纯 rubric 奖励能提高覆盖，但会放松支持性；乘法式 gating 会继承 grounding 的保守缺点；简单加法能缓解但不完全；一个“软组合”——非 gating 的 grounding 项 + rubric coverage + pairwise relevance——在当前实验里给出最平衡的结果。\n\nrubric 的构建也有方法细节。作者不是让单个模型写 rubric，而是用多生成器 union pipeline：先由 GPT-5.4 和 Gemini-3-Flash-Preview 独立生成候选 rubric，再由 GPT-OSS-120B 合并、去重，得到最终版本。这样做是为了减少单一 rubric writer 的遗漏，并把覆盖面做宽，但同时限制 rubric 大小，避免 judge 成本过高。训练数据方面，RL pool 共 4K prompts，其中 2K 是带 context 的 grounded prompts，2K 是来自 WildChat 的 open prompts，用来避免模型只在 reference-grounded 场景里学会保守策略。\n\n从方法取舍看，这篇论文的关键不是更强的 RL 算法，而是 reward specification。它把“有用的长答案”拆成两个可分离目标：句子是否被支持，以及问题要求的信息是否被覆盖。相比长度、claim count、detail reward，这种定义更接近真实用户需求，也更容易解释 reward trade-off 为什么出现。","experiments":"实验在两个开源模型家族上做：Qwen3-4B 和 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B。作者控制变量比较严格：同一 base checkpoint、同一 prompt pool、同一 rollout 配置、同一评测切分和 judge，只换 reward。\n\n训练集是 4,000 个 RL prompts：2,000 个 grounded prompts 带 context，来自已有 reliability-oriented pool；2,000 个 open prompts 来自 WildChat。评测覆盖 FACTS Grounding、LongFact、FActScore closed-book / open-book，以及 CL-bench 这类 checklist 风格任务，还看 creative-writing 和 pairwise preference 之类的 OOD 行为。\n\n主要实验结论很稳定。第一，FACT-ONLY 这类严格 grounding reward 会在很少的 RL steps 内显著缩短回答，模型学到的策略是通过少答来规避 unsupported claims。第二，RUBRIC-ONLY 能更好保持 coverage，尤其在 checklist completion 和开放式写作上迁移更好，但 grounding 会下降。第三，硬 gating 的 fact-rubric 组合在 OOD 上仍然偏弱，因为 gating 机制保留了“宁可不说”的偏好。第四，soft three-component reward 在 in-distribution grounding 与 OOD completion / preference 之间取得更平衡的结果。\n\n从全文摘录能确认的硬数字主要是模型规模和训练集规模。摘要没有给出完整分数表，因此这里不虚构具体提升幅度。更重要的实验洞察是 reward 形状决定了策略形状：一旦 grounding 以全有或全无的方式进入目标，模型就容易把 refusal 当成最稳妥解。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先看 Introduction 里对 refusal-to-richness trade-off 的定义，再看 reward design 那一节，弄清楚 rubric coverage、grounding 和 pairwise relevance 各自约束什么。之后直接看主结果表，重点不是谁分数最高，而是不同 reward 组合在 ID grounding 与 OOD checklist / creative writing 上的方向性差异。\n\n如果只花 25 分钟，建议看四处：1）rubric 的形式化定义；2）五种 reward 的公式或表格；3）FACT-ONLY 导致回答长度快速下降的训练曲线；4）OOD 评测结果。值得深挖的点有三个：一是 rubric 作为 reward 是否会过拟合 rubric writer 的偏好；二是多生成器 union 是否足够稳定；三是这种 reward 设计能否迁移到 reasoning RL、RAG answer generation，甚至 continual post-training。对做 pretrain 接口的人，最有用的启发是：训练信号如果只惩罚错误，不奖励完整性，模型会自然收缩输出支持域。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a specific failure mode in long-form hallucination RL: if the reward focuses too much on grounding, the model learns to say less; if the reward focuses too much on richness, unsupported content comes back. The proposed fix is to define richness not with global proxies such as length or claim count, but with question-specific key-point rubrics that measure whether the answer actually covers what the prompt asks for.","importance":"This is more post-training than pretraining, but it is highly relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface because it shows how reward design reshapes the output distribution. The useful lesson is not merely that rubrics beat length. It is that long-form RL needs an explicit notion of information coverage; otherwise grounding rewards induce the wrong optimum, namely conservative under-answering. That matters for RLHF/RLAIF, synthetic supervision, continual post-training, and any setup where reliability and completeness must coexist.","technical_approach":"$24","experiments":"$25","reading_guide":"$26","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读它不是为“又一个RL配方”，而是看 rubric 把 richness 从 proxy 变成可监督目标；重点看 trade-off 曲线与组合权重设定。","en":"Not about a new RL recipe—rubrics turn “richness” from a proxy into a trainable target; focus on the trade-off curves and how they weight combined rewards."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Peking University","State Key Laboratory of Multimedia Information Processing"],"authors":["Yudong Wang","Zhe Yang","Wenhan Ma","Rang Li","Qibin Yang","Weimin Xiong","Jiangshan Duo","Liang Zhao","Zhifang Sui"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["rlhf","reward-modeling","hallucination","long-form-generation","rubric"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12337","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.12337","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.13387","title":"CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation","abstract":"On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. t","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T15:48:16Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13387.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 往语义依赖方向走了一步。对做蒸馏、在线 SFT、RFT 前的教师监督分配的人，这个视角有参考价值；但如果关心的是大规模预训练 recipe，本工作更像局部机制改进，而不是主线进展。","technical_approach":"$27","experiments":"实验覆盖两个 teacher-student 设置，并把 CROP 放到统一的 budgeted OPD 框架里，与非 CROP 的强基线做直接比较。摘要里给出的主结果是：CROP 相比最强的非 CROP selector，aggregate performance 分别提升 1.92 和 2.96 分。这个幅度对 selective distillation 这类局部监督分配问题来说是有意义的，说明 token 选择标准确实会影响最终能力，而不是只影响训练效率。\n\n更有信息量的是 token-selection control 实验。作者把高 relevance、随机、低 relevance 三种 token 选择方式放在匹配预算下比较，结果呈现稳定排序：选最高 relevance 的 token 最好，随机居中，最低 relevance 明显更差。这个实验很关键，因为它比单纯和若干 heuristic baseline 比较更能说明 relevance score 本身有判别力，而不是靠某个实现细节偶然奏效。\n\n组件消融方面，论文结论是 counterfactual sensitivity 是主要信号，paraphrase calibration 还能带来额外收益。也就是说，如果只看“对关键条件变化是否敏感”，已经能抓到一部分高价值 token；再减去“对表面改写也敏感”的部分，可以进一步过滤掉形式层面的伪相关位置。这个结果和方法动机是一致的。文中还把 CREDIT 适配到相同预算选择器下做对照，目的是避免比较时 selector 机制不同带来的干扰。\n\n从研究者视角，实验仍有几个边界。第一，提升是 aggregate score，若想判断方法是否更适合 reasoning、factual QA、还是 instruction following，需要细看任务分解。第二，triplet 构造依赖离线生成和验证，实际成本与可扩展性需要结合附录看。第三，方法默认存在可定义的“material condition”可被 counterfactual 改写，这在开放式对话或长文生成里未必总是容易自动化。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓定义，再看证据。优先看 Introduction 里对 optimization need 与 task relevance 评分上没有统计显著差异；人工评估者并排比较也未发现质量差异。局限包括：短文本信号不足；事实性段落和代码可操作空间小；Claude 仅轻度编辑人类文本时，水印可能附着的 token 太少；检测器只能给出 Claude 参与的似然，不能证明文本为人类所写，也不能直接识别其他 AI 系统。","reading_guide":"博文较短，适合完整读一遍。重点关注三段：低风险 token hidden characters, or increasing cost, while meeting EU AI Act content-marking requirements.","importance":"This is not a pretraining objective or architecture change, but it modifies generation at the sampling layer for a major model family. That matters for generated-data corpora, third-party detection, evaluation contamination, and any analysis of model-output distributions. For foundation-model researchers, the relevant questions are whether keyed sampling introduces measurable biases in long-form, factual, or code generation, and how it interacts with temperature, top-p, deterministic decoding, and post-editing.","technical_approach":"Claude uses a SynthID-Text-style method from Google DeepMind, in the line of Scott Aaronson’s 2022 watermarking proposal. The model still chooses among plausible next tokens, but at low-stakes positions where several candidates are nearly equally good, the ordinary RNG is replaced by pseudorandomness derived from a secret key and preceding tokens. A detector with the key can test whether the sequence of choices is statistically consistent with that keyed process and estimate the probability that Claude contributed. The method does not force unusual words or change meaning. In factual passages, code, or light proofreading where few alternatives are acceptable, the watermark becomes sparse or inactive.","experiments":"Anthropic reports no internal impact on content, creativity, or readability. The post cites SynthID-Text’s Gemini A/B test, which found no statistically significant difference in thumbs-up/thumbs-down ratings between watermarked and unwatermarked models, and controlled human side-by-side evaluations found no quality difference. Limitations include weak signal on short text, sparse signal in factual or code generation, weak detectability when Claude only lightly edits human text, and the fact that detection estimates Claude involvement rather than proving human authorship or identifying another AI system.","reading_guide":"Read the full post; it is short. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction. ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T17:42:20Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12447.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 Transformer residual stream 中的“特权基”到底对应什么功能方向。核心结论是：当前预测 token 的 unembedding 方向不仅是 logit readout 的诊断轴，也是 residual stream 几何结构的锚点。围绕这个方向，模型把少量与读出直接相关的变化压缩在很窄的 prediction interface 中，同时把大部分 residual variation 放在远离 readout 的补空间里。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在机制分析和训练动态解释。论文提示，AR 训练下的线性 unembedding readout 会在 residual stream 中形成稳定的几何分层：预测相关信息需要靠近读出方向，其他计算状态则尽量保持远离，以免干扰 logits。这有助于理解为什么宽模型增加了大量维度，但直接服务于当前 token readout 的有效维度并不随宽度线性扩大。它也给 logit lens、activation patching、表示压缩、残差流正则化等分析方法提供了更具体的几何坐标系。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 prediction-anchored decomposition，作者称为 PDSF。给定某个位置的 residual state，先取当前 argmax token 的 unembedding 向量作为 prediction direction，并将 residual state 分解出 rank-1 投影 P。然后在去掉 P 后的残差上做 PCA，取高方差子空间 D；再在去掉 P+D 后的残差上做第二轮 PCA，取子空间 S；剩余正交补空间记为 F。P+D+S 被定义为 prediction interface，F 是 prediction-distal complement。这个设计和普通 PCA 的差别在于，它先固定一个由模型自身预测决定的功能轴，再看方差如何围绕该轴分层，而不是只找全局高方差方向。论文进一步比较不同 prompt set 下各子空间的有效维度、方差分布、读出相关性和对生成的作用。","experiments":"实验覆盖 18 个 Transformer 模型，来自 Llama、Gemma、Mistral、Mixtral、Qwen、GPT-OSS 六类模型族，包含 dense 与 MoE、base 与 instruction-tuned，参数规模约 7B 到 120B。作者在三个结构化 prompt sets 上分析 residual stream。主要结果是：在受约束的一 token 回答任务中，prediction-proximal 的 D 子空间有效维度通常只有个位数，整个 prediction interface 的有效维度少于 20；这个窄接口在不同模型宽度上近似保持稳定。与此同时，F 补空间占据绝大部分维度，每个方向与 readout 的对齐较弱，但并非无用噪声，而是对时间推进和后续生成有因果作用。一个关键观察是 residual stream 的变化更多体现为方向组织，而不是单纯的范数变化。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 introduction 和方法部分的 PDSF 定义，再看实验图中关于 effective dimensionality、variance proximity、prediction interface 宽度随模型规模变化的结果。重点不是具体 PCA 技巧，而是“先固定当前预测 token 的 unembedding 方向，再分析 residual geometry”的框架。预计阅读 30 到 45 分钟。若做 interpretability 或 pretrain dynamics，可以深挖两个问题：第一，prediction interface 的宽度为何随模型宽度近似不变；第二，F 补空间如何在远离 readout 的同时仍保留对未来 token 的因果信息。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what functional role the privileged basis in transformer residual streams plays. Its main claim is that the unembedding direction of the token currently predicted by the model is not only a diagnostic readout axis, but also a geometric anchor. Residual-stream variation stratifies by proximity to this prediction direction: a small prediction interface carries readout-relevant structure, while most variation lives in a prediction-distal complement.","importance":"The result is useful for understanding pretraining dynamics and residual-stream organization. Autoregressive training with a fixed linear unembedding appears to induce a narrow geometric channel through which computation becomes legible to logits. Increasing model width does not simply expand the number of dimensions directly used for current-token readout; instead, much of the added capacity lies in directions weakly aligned with the unembedding. This has practical implications for logit-lens analysis, activation patching, representation compression, and hypotheses about how wide models separate computation from readout.","technical_approach":"The main tool is a prediction-anchored decomposition called PDSF. For each residual state, the method first takes the unembedding vector of the current argmax token as the prediction direction and extracts the rank-1 projection P. It then performs PCA on the residual after removing P to obtain a high-variance subspace D, performs another PCA after removing P+D to obtain S, and treats the remaining orthogonal subspace as F. The union P+D+S is called the prediction interface, while F is the prediction-distal complement. This differs from ordinary PCA because the first axis is not chosen by variance; it is chosen by the model’s own readout geometry.","experiments":"The study evaluates 18 transformer models from six families: Llama, Gemma, Mistral, Mixtral, Qwen, and GPT-OSS. The suite includes dense and MoE models, base and instruction-tuned variants, and sizes from roughly 7B to 120B parameters. Across three structured prompt sets, the prediction-proximal region remains small. On constrained one-token-answer prompts, the D subspace has single-digit effective dimensionality, and the full prediction interface has fewer than about 20 effective dimensions. This narrowness is approximately invariant to model width. The F complement contains most dimensions and is weakly aligned with readout on a per-direction basis, but it remains causally and temporally important for generation.","reading_guide":"Skim the introduction for the geometric framing, then read the PDSF method carefully. Our contributions are to establish the first complete characterization of which regular languages transformers length-generalize on and provide a decision algorithm running ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T16:20:56Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13433.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.FL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer 究竟在哪些正则语言上能长度泛化？此前缺少完整刻画，连 C-RASP（被经验证据认为对应 transformer 长度泛化能力的形式语言）能表示哪些正则语言都没有判定算法。","importance":"属于 transformer 表达力与长度泛化的基础理论工作。对 pretrain 研究者的价值偏间接：它把'哪些状态追踪任务能被 transformer 学会并泛化到更长序列'从经验观察推进到可判定的代数条件，有助于理解长上下文泛化、结构化约束（JSON/代码括号）、agent 工作流状态追踪等任务的内在可学性边界。","technical_approach":"核心是为 C-RASP 建立一套类比 Krohn-Rhodes 的代数分解理论。Krohn-Rhodes 的 flip-flop 和简单群构件在 C-RASP 中不可表达，而 C-RASP 的无界计数单元又超出有限半群的刻画范围，因此经典工具失效。作者引入整数加法群作为新的构件，发展出对应的代数分解理论，从而完整刻画 C-RASP 中的正则语言，并给出一个在语言句法幺半群规模上多项式时间运行的判定算法，可判断给定 DFA 对应的语言是否属于 C-RASP。","experiments":"论文以理论结果为主。动机部分用一对结构相似的正则语言 (ab+bbaa)* 与 (ab+aabb)* 展示长度泛化行为的显著差异，指出现有理论无法解释；新刻画能区分这类情况。主要'实验'是判定算法的构造与复杂度证明，而非大规模模型训练。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：先看 Introduction 和 Figure 1 理解问题动机与那对反例；再看主定理陈述和多项式判定算法的输入输出，不必逐行跟证明。对代数自动机理论（句法幺半群、Krohn-Rhodes 分解、trivial monoid 等）不熟悉的读者可先补相关背景。预计用时 40–60 分钟。值得深挖的点：C-RASP 的无界计数单元与实际 transformer 中注意力/位置编码实现计数的对应关系，以及该判定算法能否用于预测某类结构化任务的长度泛化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Which regular languages can transformers length-generalize on? Its value to pretrain research is indirect but real: it moves 'which state-tracking tasks can a transformer learn and generalize to longer sequences' from empirical observation to a decidable algebraic condition, informing understanding of long-context generalization, structured constraints (JSON, code delimiters), and state tracking in agentic workflows.","technical_approach":"The paper develops an algebraic decomposition theory for C-RASP analogous to Krohn-Rhodes theory. The classical Krohn-Rhodes building blocks (flip-flop units and simple groups) are not expressible in C-RASP, while C-RASP's unbounded counting units are not expressible by finite semigroups, so classical tools break down. The authors introduce the additive group on the integers as a new building block and build a corresponding decomposition theory, yielding an effective characterization of the regular languages in C-RASP together with a polynomial-time decision algorithm (in the size of the language's syntactic monoid) for deciding whether a given DFA's language belongs to C-RASP.","experiments":"The contribution is primarily theoretical. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. ov","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-05-29T01:42:07Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12321.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的是：LLM available constraint information into the decision. It formalizes this as conditional constraint activation: the constraint may be linearly encoded in hidden states, may appear symmetrically in active and removed prompts, but may still fail to influence the final gold-versus-shortcut choice.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is diagnostic. The results argue against a simple “the model forgot the constraint” story and also against treating longer reasoning traces as a reliable fix. For at least one failure mode, the model contains a decodable constraint representation, yet that representation is not used by the answer circuit. This points toward training signals that contrast active and removed cases, penalize over-triggering, and supervise conditional routing rather than merely rewarding explicit prerequisite mentions or longer chains of thought.","technical_approach":"The method is built around a quartet diagnostic. Each scenario is converted into Active, Removed, Explicit, and Salience Control variants. This separates genuine constraint use from conservative defaulting and from generic salience effects. The formal test has four parts: Knowledge, measured by a linear probe for whether the constraint applies; Symmetry, checking whether probe accuracy is comparable across active and removed prompts; Routing, checking whether the probe-projected magnitude predicts the gold-shortcut logit gap; and Repair, testing whether activation patching from an explicit-constraint donor can fix active failures without flipping removed controls. The paper also uses a salience-controlled hint ladder to distinguish semantic constraint cues from length- or attention-driven hint effects.","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"A useful skim takes about 25 to 35 minutes. The most informative comparison is Qwen3-14B versus GPT-OSS-20B, because it separates prior-biased over-activation from a routing failure. In this work, we ask whether language identity is merely linearly decodable from hidden states, or if it can be causally controlled by a compact activation direction. We conduct an exhaustive causal intervention analysis across multiple model families, including Qwen 3.5-2B and Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct, isolating PCA-derived \"language axes\" to perform","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-06-02T23:32:01Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_12334.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“语言能否从隐藏状态里线性读出”，而是更强的问题：多语模型在生成时选择哪种语言，是否由一个可干预、低维、层相关的内部方向控制。作者把问题从相关性 probing 推到因果干预：如果某个“语言轴”真在生成中起作用，那么沿这个方向做加法或消融，输出语言应当系统性改变，而不是只让模型退化或随机扰动。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于提出新训练方法，而在于把“多语能力如何在表征中组织”这件事讲得更具体。它给出一个较清楚的 mechanistic 结论：语言身份不是弥散在全网、不可操作的统计痕迹，而是至少部分落在残差流中的紧凑方向上，并且这种方向具有明显的语言对依赖和层依赖。对多语预训练、语言控制、跨语对齐和 activation engineering 都有启发。量级上，它更像一篇扎实的机制分析与推理时控制论文，而不是会立刻改写 pretrain recipe 的工作。","technical_approach":"方法核心很直接，但设计比较严谨。作者在冻结模型权重的前提下，只在推理时改 hidden states。模型包括 Qwen 3.5-2B（24 层，d=2048）和 Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct（16 层，d=2048）。\n\n语言轴的构造分三步。第一步，用 2 万个 held-out 双语句对作为校准集，从每一层抽取 hidden states，并做 layerwise mean-centering。第二步，在每层对激活做 PCA，保留前 150 个主成分。第三步，不是直接取第一主成分，而是计算每个主成分上两种语言分布的 Cohen’s d，选取语言分离效应最大的那个分量，并统一方向，使正方向对应目标语言。这样得到每层一个 unit vector 作为 language axis。这个选择比“均值差向量”或“probe 权重”更稳，因为它先限制在主要方差子空间里，再显式优化语言分离度，减少噪声维度和过拟合校准集的问题。\n\n干预分两类：steering 和 ablation。steering 是在前向过程中对某层残差流加上沿语言轴的偏移，测试能否把输出推向目标语言；ablation 则是去掉 hidden state 在该轴上的投影，测试该方向是否对原本的语言生成有必要性。作者还做了大量对照，包括等幅随机方向扰动、不同层位点、跨脚本与同脚本语言对比较，用来排除“任何大扰动都能改输出”这种解释。\n\n论文里一个重要观察是，语言轴不是通用的“语言性”方向，而是 pair-specific 的。英中轴和英西轴并不能简单互换，说明模型内部更像是若干语言对相关的局部几何，而不是单一全局语言坐标。另一个关键点是层间行为不均匀：中层往往对 steering 有阻力，晚层更敏感，提示语言选择不是在最末端才决定，但最终表面形式的锁定更依赖后层表示。","experiments":"实验规模对这类 activation intervention 工作算比较大。主实验在 FLORES-200 上做，英中使用 500 个句对，英西使用 100 个句对；总计进行了 126 万次生成。语言轴校准来自 MultiUN 的 2 万个 held-out 句对。\n\n关键结果有几类。第一，沿 PCA+Cohen’s d 选出的语言轴做 steering，能够稳定触发语言切换，既适用于跨脚本的 English→Chinese，也适用于同脚本的 English→Spanish；而等幅随机扰动通常不能复现这种效果，说明不是一般性的表示破坏。第二，ablation 会削弱或破坏目标语言生成，支持该方向不仅可控，而且被模型实际使用。第三，层扫描显示存在明显的 layerwise window：部分中层即使加较强 steering 也不容易改写输出语言，而后层更容易把语言表面形式推向目标侧。第四，作者区分了“有效 steering”和“生成崩坏”的边界，说明干预强度过大时会损伤流畅性与一致性，因此真正可用的是一段有限的、coherent 的干预区间。\n\n从给出的全文内容看，论文更强调现象稳定性和控制实验，而不是追求单一 benchmark 数字。摘要里没有列出完整的切换率、困惑度或质量分数表，因此这里能确认的硬数字主要是模型规模、层数、校准数据量、评测样本量和总生成次数。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Method，抓住它与线性 probe 工作的区别：重点不是“能读出来”，而是“改了以后会不会变”。然后看 layerwise intervention 的主结果图，通常是最有信息量的部分，能直接看到哪些层可控、哪些层有阻力。再看随机方向对照和 ablation 结果，这两部分决定结论是否站得住。\n\n如果只花 20 到 30 分钟，建议按这个顺序：1）方法里 language axis 的构造；2）主 steering 结果；3）随机扰动对照；4）不同语言对和不同层的比较。值得深挖的点有两个：一是为什么 pair-specific 轴比共享语言轴更有效，这关系到多语表征几何；二是中层阻力与后层敏感性是否对应语义到表层形式的分工。如果你关心 pretrain 方向，可以进一步想：这种几何是否受 tokenizer、语料配比、指令微调或多语对齐目标影响。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a stronger question than linear decodability: when a multilingual LM chooses its output language, is that choice controlled by a compact, causally active direction in hidden space? The goal is to move from correlation to intervention. If a “language axis” is truly used by the model during generation, then adding or removing that component in the residual stream should systematically change the output language, rather than merely degrading the model.","importance":"Its value for pretraining research is mostly mechanistic, not recipe-changing. The main contribution is a clearer picture of how multilingual structure is organized internally: language identity appears to be at least partly encoded in compact residual-stream directions that are layer-specific and language-pair-specific. That matters for multilingual pretraining, controllable generation, activation engineering, and for thinking about whether multilingual models build language-neutral semantic hubs or retain pairwise geometric structure. distribution 和生成风格，而且这种变化未必带来更好的 calibration。对做对齐、蒸馏、reward modeling、以及把 verbalized confidence 当训练信号的人，这篇工作有参考意义。它说明“更会回答”与“更会显得确定”是两件事，后者还可能伴随 rationale 生成分布收缩。","technical_approach":"方法上是一个成对比较分析。作者选了三组 base / instruction-tuned 配对模型：Qwen2.5-7B、Llama-3.1-8B、Mistral-7B-v0.3，并在 ARC-Easy、MMLU、CommonsenseQA 三个多选 benchmark 上评估。置信度用了两类互补指标。第一类是 likelihood-based answer uncertainty：把各候选答案的条件概率归一化后，计算 choice entropy，熵越低表示模型越确定。第二类是 verbalized confidence：先用模型 likelihood 选出答案，再在第二次前向中要求模型给出该答案正确的数值概率。这样可以区分“内部偏好分布更尖锐”和“口头上更自信”这两种现象。\n\nrationale 多样性方面，作者对每个问题采样 K=5 条 chain-of-thought rationale，用 Unique-2 和 Self-BLEU 衡量。Unique-2 看不同 bigram 的比例，偏向词汇丰富度；1-SelfBLEU 看同一问题多次生成之间的差异，偏向跨样本变异度。论文还做了一个重要控制：只在 base 和 instruct 模型给出相同最终答案的样本上比较，并匹配 rationale 长度，尽量把“答案换了”或“生成更长了”这些混杂因素拿掉。这个设计是全文最扎实的部分，因为否则 diversity 的变化很容易只是由 answer switching 或长度效应造成。\n\n从研究设计看，这篇工作不是在证明 instruction tuning 导致某种单一机制，而是在拆开几个常被混在一起的现象：准确率、likelihood calibration、verbalized confidence、以及 rationale lexical diversity。它的价值在于把这些量分开测，而不是只看一个 aggregate 指标。","experiments":"主结果很一致：instruction tuning 在所有模型和 benchmark 上都提高了“看起来更确定”的程度，但准确率提升并不稳定，likelihood-based calibration 也没有同步改善。文中给出的具体数字包括：Qwen 在 MMLU 上的 answer entropy 从 0.430 降到 0.131，Mistral 在 CSQA 上从 0.736 降到 0.268；Llama 在 ARC-Easy 上 verbalized confidence 从 49.2% 升到 90.4%，在 CSQA 上从 46.8% 升到 91.1%。但 ARC-Easy 上 Llama 的准确率保持 82.2% 不变，说明“更自信”并不等于“更准”。\n\nrationale 多样性结果则明显更复杂。Unique-2 的变化不统一，有的模型/任务下降，有的上升；例如 Mistral 在 CSQA 上 Unique-2 从 0.719 升到 0.750，但在 ARC-Easy 和 MMLU 上下降更明显。相比之下，1-SelfBLEU 在所有模型和 benchmark 上都下降，也就是多次生成的 rationale 彼此更相似、跨样本变异更小。最大下降出现在 Mistral 的 ARC-Easy，从 0.813 降到 0.626。这个结果说明 instruction tuning 更稳定地压缩了 rationale 的生成分布，但不一定统一降低 token-level lexical richness。\n\n论文进一步做了受控分析，考察在相同答案、匹配长度条件下，置信度变化是否和 diversity 变化一致。结论是否定的：更低的不确定性或更高的 verbalized confidence，并不稳定对应更低或更高的 lexical diversity。这一点很重要，因为它反驳了一个直觉假设：模型之所以更自信，是因为它学会了更固定、更模板化的解释。数据只支持“跨次生成更收敛”，不支持“所有层面的词汇多样性都同步收缩”。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读。先看 Introduction 和 Methodology，明确作者区分了哪几种 confidence 与 diversity 指标。随后重点看 Table 1 和受控比较部分，尤其是 same-answer、length-matched 的分析，因为那部分最能排除表面混杂因素。若只想抓结论，读主文 20 分钟足够；若想把它用于自己的 post-train 评估设计，再花 10 到 15 分钟看附录里的 prompting、采样和 verbalized confidence elicitation 细节。\n\n值得带走的不是某个具体数值，而是评估框架：以后比较 base 与 instruct、SFT 与 preference-tuned、或不同 RL 阶段模型时，最好把 accuracy、choice entropy、verbalized confidence、以及 rationale diversity 分开看。否则很容易把风格变化误判成能力或校准变化。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what instruction tuning actually changes about model confidence. It focuses on two linked questions: whether the higher confidence often seen after instruction tuning reflects real gains in predictive accuracy or merely stronger certainty in the output distribution, and whether those confidence shifts are accompanied by systematic changes in the lexical diversity of generated rationales.","importance":"This is primarily an analysis paper rather than a training-method contribution, so its direct impact on pretraining is limited. That matters for anyone using verbalized confidence, rationale consistency, or self-reported certainty as a training or evaluation signal in post-training pipelines.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"This is a good skim paper. The injection is norm matched: the decoder direction is normalized, scaled to the average norm of the hidden states in the injection span, and controlled by a strength parameter α. This reduces confounding from raw direction norms. Unlike activation-example methods, the feature-specific input is only the decoder direction; no retrieved texts are supplied at inference time.","experiments":"The reported experiments show generalization to unseen SAE features, transfer across independently trained SAE dictionaries, and extension to features from other LLMs through a small adapter. It views demonstrations as inducing a compact f","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-13T15:46:50Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_13385.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有隐式多模态上下文学习（implicit M-ICL）方法设计跨度极大，从静态任务向量到查询条件变换、注意力路由均有涉及，但缺乏统一理论解释不同任务到底需要多大复杂度的干预，无法预测简单任务向量何时足够、何时必须引入更复杂的设计。","importance":"将ICL的内部作用机制与演示压缩干预的设计直接关联，为多模态模型的ICL能力利用、参数高效适配、推理时演示压缩提供了统一分析框架，跳出了盲目堆叠方法复杂度的研究路径，给出了可实证检验的理论假设。","technical_approach":"提出Selection-Realization（选择-实现）假说，把隐式M-ICL的干预复杂度拆为两个独立维度：一是选择复杂度，即干预是跨查询静态复用还是随输入条件变化；二是实现复杂度，即干预在模型内的实现方式，从局部加性偏移、跨注意力/MLP分布式变化到注意力路由逐层递进。两个维度构成嵌套的干预层级，将此前异构的各类方法统一到同一框架下。验证实验采用受控多模态任务，在保持任务基元和提示格式不变的前提下仅调整查询依赖程度，通过对比正确演示与匹配反事实演示诱导的计算差异检验假说。","experiments":"核心发现包括：演示诱导的内部变化是紧凑的，但并非总在查询间共享；这些变化在查询维度和计算位点维度的变异程度，可同时预测静态任务向量的失效场景以及需要补充的灵活性类型。该诊断方法在自然任务上同样有效，可支持成本感知的方法选择，无需默认采用最复杂的干预设计。","reading_guide":"泛读重点关注第1节的问题定义与选择-实现假说的核心逻辑（图1对现有方法的统一分类直观清晰），以及第3节的受控实验设计思路，预计阅读时间20-30分钟。若研究方向为ICL机制或参数高效适配，可进一步精读第4节的诊断指标与反事实分析方法。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Existing implicit multimodal in-context learning (M-ICL) methods span a wide design space from static task vectors to query-conditioned transformations and attention routing, but no unifying account exists to determine how much intervention complexity is actually required for a given task. It remains unpredictable when a simple task vector is sufficient and when more complex designs are necessary.","importance":"This work directly connects the internal mechanism of ICL to the design of compressed demonstration interventions, providing a unified analytical framework for leveraging ICL capabilities in multimodal models, parameter-efficient adaptation, and inference-time demonstration compression. It moves beyond the paradigm of blindly increasing method complexity, and offers an empirically testable theoretical hypothesis.","technical_approach":"The Selection–Realization Hypothesis is put forward, which decomposes the complexity of implicit M-ICL interventions into two independent dimensions: (1) Selection complexity: whether the intervention is reused across queries or varies conditional on the input; (2) Realization complexity: how the intervention is implemented within the model, ranging from localized additive shifts, to coordinated changes across attention and MLP components, to attention routing. These dimensions form a nested intervention hierarchy that unifies previously heterogeneous methods. Validation uses controlled multimodal tasks where query dependence is varied while holding task primitives and prompt format fixed, and compares computations induced by correct demonstrations versus matched counterfactuals to test the hypothesis.","experiments":"Key findings include: demonstration-induced internal changes are compact but not always shared across queries; the degree of variation across queries and computational sites predicts both when static task vectors fail and what type of additional flexibility is useful. It treats sequence length, model depth, attention head count, numerical precision, positional encoding, attention formulation, and masking strategy as explicit computational resources, maps the attention+FFN layer structure to circuit classes with constrained gate types, fan-in, depth, and uniformity, and synthesizes existing provable upper and lower bounds to establish a capability hierarchy across Transformer variants.","experiments":"As a theoretical survey, it contains no original experiments. Key established results synthesized include: 1) Causal-masked decoder Transformers are strictly less expressive than unmasked encoders for language recognition tasks; 2) Standard softmax attention (SMAT), the variant used in nearly all production LLMs, is strictly more expressive than two common hard attention variants (UHAT, AHAT); 3) Bounded numerical precision and additional decoding steps (e.g. chain-of-thought) fundamentally shift the upper bound of model capability; 4) Constant-depth Transformer capability maps directly to specific symmetric circuit classes, with depth as the primary resource governing expressive power.","reading_guide":"Suitable for skimming. Existing work treats these failures as secondary side effects of poor general mathematical reasoning, and does not treat basic numeracy as a distinct research object. No unified analytical framework exists to separate basic numeracy from high-level mathematical cognition, or to systematically map failure modes, root causes, and mitigation paths.","importance":"These numeracy failures trace back to fundamental pretrain design choices including tokenizer construction, numeric representation in training data, and the model's native capacity for algorithmic execution—issues that cannot be resolved by post-training on advanced math benchmarks alone. second, lack of website-specific priors leads to hallucinated infeasible tasks, or forces task descriptions to be rewritten as procedural step lists to match collected trajectories instead of retaining natural user request form, reducing synthesized data quality and diversity and limiting downstream cross-website generalization.","importance":"It proposes a website-prior centered task-trajectory co-synthesis paradigm that addresses the high annotation cost and poor cross-website generalization pain points of web agent training. The method improves both efficiency and quality of synthesized training data, and offers a useful reference for environment prior injection and self-supervised training data synthesis for general-purpose agents.","technical_approach":"SynWeaver runs in three sequential stages: 1) Structured website exploration: a DFS-based crawler constructs a functional website topology graph G=(S,T), where nodes S represent functionally distinct page states (paired with screenshots and accessibility trees) and edges T represent executable interactions, eliminating redundant traversal common in random walks while ensuring full functional coverage; 2) Website prior learning: UI supervision signals extracted from the topology graph are used to fine-tune a UI-aware model, so task proposals are grounded in global website knowledge rather than only local page observations; 3) Collaborative task-trajectory synthesis: a teacher model jointly refines task descriptions and execution prefixes during rollout, correcting mismatches between intent and execution in real time to preserve natural user request form while ensuring trajectory executability.","experiments":"Validated on WebArena and WebVoyager benchmarks: using only 822 validated task-trajectory pairs synthesized from website topology graphs, it achieves the best overall WebArena success rate on both Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and InternVL3-8B base models; on WebVoyager, it reaches a 27.06% success rate, 4.64 percentage points higher than the strongest baseline. Ablation studies confirm the necessity of all three modules. Can a scalable world model replace the real environment, and how can its prediction bias and noise be controlled so policy convergence is preserved?","importance":"Practically relevant to scaling agentic post-training, especially where execution cost dwarfs generation cost—SWE agents, data-science agents, VLA, and AutoResearch. The core observation about asymmetric cost scaling between generation and execution is general. Using a world model as a batchable, cheap reward signal, then explicitly modeling its bias and noise in a policy-gradient convergence bound and pairing them with debiasing and inverse-variance denoising, offers a useful template for large-scale agent RL pipelines. Direct relevance to base pretraining is limited; the main value is on the post-train / RL-signal-quality side.","technical_approach":"Proposes WMRL: a world model takes environment information and the agent's solution and simulates the execution outcome in a few forward passes, replacing sandbox runs so the cost batches across trajectories like generation. Imperfection is modeled as a reward bias b (|b|≤B) plus zero-mean noise ξ (standard deviation σ). Tracing both through a GRPO-style policy gradient yields two extra terms in the convergence bound, O(B²) and O(σ²). Online Debiasing recalibrates world-model outputs to offset bias, and Inverse-Variance Denoising weights signals to minimize variance; a theorem shows that applying both strictly improves the convergence guarantee.","experiments":"Across multiple AutoResearch tasks at two agent scales, replacing the real environment with the world model accelerates overall training by roughly 3–4×. With Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, the accelerated training matches or exceeds RL trained against the real environment. The method is also extended to VLA post-training to show generality. Concrete task scores and model sizes are not in the excerpt; see Section 5 of the paper.","reading_guide":"Skim.
